'reality control ?? ?? ?? it is called ?? though ?? comprises much else as well ?? ?? means the power of holding two contradictory beliefs in one ?? mind simultaneously ?? and accepting both of them ?? ?? ?? intellectual knows in which direction his memories must be altered ?? he therefore knows that he is playing tricks with reality ?? but by the exercise of ?? he also satisfies himself that reality is not violated ??
